Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the stability space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 813 and the convergence rate is O(406/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_813(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 813. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^406(S, F) is trivial. QED.
